28/* SecTAG length = macsec_eth_header without the optional SCI */
29#define MACSEC_TAG_LEN6 6
30
31struct macsec_eth_header {
32 struct ethhdr eth;
33 /* SecTAG */
34 u8 tci_an;
35#if defined(__LITTLE_ENDIAN_BITFIELD)
36 u8 short_length:6,
37 unused:2;
38#elif defined(__BIG_ENDIAN_BITFIELD)
39 u8 unused:2,
40 short_length:6;
41#else
42#error "Please fix <asm/byteorder.h>"
43#endif
44 __be32 packet_number;
45 u8 secure_channel_id[8]; /* optional */
46} __packed__attribute__((__packed__));
47
48/* minimum secure data length deemed "not short", see IEEE 802.1AE-2006 9.7 */
49#define MIN_NON_SHORT_LEN48 48

87/**
88 * struct macsec_dev - private data
89 * @secy: SecY config
90 * @real_dev: pointer to underlying netdevice
91 * @dev_tracker: refcount tracker for @real_dev reference
92 * @stats: MACsec device stats
93 * @secys: linked list of SecY's on the underlying device
94 * @gro_cells: pointer to the Generic Receive Offload cell
95 * @offload: status of offloading on the MACsec device
96 * @insert_tx_tag: when offloading, device requires to insert an
97 * additional tag
98 */
99struct macsec_dev {
100 struct macsec_secy secy;
101 struct net_device *real_dev;
102 netdevice_tracker dev_tracker;
103 struct pcpu_secy_stats __percpu *stats;
104 struct list_head secys;
105 struct gro_cells gro_cells;
106 enum macsec_offload offload;
107 bool insert_tx_tag;
108};
109
110/**
111 * struct macsec_rxh_data - rx_handler private argument
112 * @secys: linked list of SecY's on this underlying device
113 */
114struct macsec_rxh_data {
115 struct list_head secys;
116};
117
118static struct macsec_dev *macsec_priv(const struct net_device *dev)
119{
120 return (struct macsec_dev *)netdev_priv(dev);
121}

165static struct macsec_rx_sc *macsec_rxsc_get(struct macsec_rx_sc *sc)

170static void macsec_rxsc_put(struct macsec_rx_sc *sc)
171{

205{

262static unsigned int macsec_sectag_len(bool sci_present)
263{
264 return MACSEC_TAG_LEN6 + (sci_present ? MACSEC_SCI_LEN8 : 0);
265}
266
267static unsigned int macsec_hdr_len(bool sci_present)
268{
269 return macsec_sectag_len(sci_present) + ETH_HLEN14;
270}
271
272static unsigned int macsec_extra_len(bool sci_present)
273{
274 return macsec_sectag_len(sci_present) + sizeof(__be16);
275}

303 else if (secy->icv_len != MACSEC_DEFAULT_ICV_LEN16)
304 h->tci_an |= MACSEC_TCI_C0x04;

307}
308
309static void macsec_set_shortlen(struct macsec_eth_header *h, size_t data_len)
310{
311 if (data_len < MIN_NON_SHORT_LEN48)
312 h->short_length = data_len;
313}
314
315/* Checks if a MACsec interface is being offloaded to an hardware engine */
316static bool macsec_is_offloaded(struct macsec_dev *macsec)
317{
318 if (macsec->offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_MAC ||
319 macsec->offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
320 return true;
321
322 return false;
323}
324
325/* Checks if underlying layers implement MACsec offloading functions. */
326static bool macsec_check_offload(enum macsec_offload offload,
327 struct macsec_dev *macsec)
328{
329 if (!macsec || !macsec->real_dev)
330 return false;
331
332 if (offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
333 return macsec->real_dev->phydev &&
334 macsec->real_dev->phydev->macsec_ops;
335 else if (offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_MAC)
336 return macsec->real_dev->features & NETIF_F_HW_MACSEC((netdev_features_t)1 << (NETIF_F_HW_MACSEC_BIT)) &&
337 macsec->real_dev->macsec_ops;
338
339 return false;
340}
341
342static const struct macsec_ops *__macsec_get_ops(enum macsec_offload offload,
343 struct macsec_dev *macsec,
344 struct macsec_context *ctx)
345{
346 if (ctx) {
347 memset(ctx, 0, sizeof(*ctx))({ size_t __fortify_size = (size_t)(sizeof(*ctx)); fortify_memset_chk
(__fortify_size, __builtin_dynamic_object_size(ctx, 0), __builtin_dynamic_object_size
(ctx, 1)), __builtin_memset(ctx, 0, __fortify_size); });
348 ctx->offload = offload;
349
350 if (offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
351 ctx->phydev = macsec->real_dev->phydev;
352 else if (offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_MAC)
353 ctx->netdev = macsec->real_dev;
354 }
355
356 if (offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
357 return macsec->real_dev->phydev->macsec_ops;
358 else
359 return macsec->real_dev->macsec_ops;
360}
361
362/* Returns a pointer to the MACsec ops struct if any and updates the MACsec
363 * context device reference if provided.
364 */
365static const struct macsec_ops *macsec_get_ops(struct macsec_dev *macsec,
366 struct macsec_context *ctx)
367{
368 if (!macsec_check_offload(macsec->offload, macsec))
369 return NULL((void *)0);
370
371 return __macsec_get_ops(macsec->offload, macsec, ctx);
372}
373
374/* validate MACsec packet according to IEEE 802.1AE-2018 9.12 */
375static bool macsec_validate_skb(struct sk_buff *skb, u16 icv_len, bool xpn)
376{
377 struct macsec_eth_header *h = (struct macsec_eth_header *)skb->data;
378 int len = skb->len - 2 * ETH_ALEN6;
379 int extra_len = macsec_extra_len(!!(h->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_SC0x20)) + icv_len;
380
381 /* a) It comprises at least 17 octets */
382 if (skb->len <= 16)
383 return false;
384
385 /* b) MACsec EtherType: already checked */
386
387 /* c) V bit is clear */
388 if (h->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_VERSION0x80)
389 return false;
390
391 /* d) ES or SCB => !SC */
392 if ((h->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_ES0x40 || h->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_SCB0x10) &&
393 (h->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_SC0x20))
394 return false;
395
396 /* e) Bits 7 and 8 of octet 4 of the SecTAG are clear */
397 if (h->unused)
398 return false;
399
400 /* rx.pn != 0 if not XPN (figure 10-5 with 802.11AEbw-2013 amendment) */
401 if (!h->packet_number && !xpn)
402 return false;
403
404 /* length check, f) g) h) i) */
405 if (h->short_length)
406 return len == extra_len + h->short_length;
407 return len >= extra_len + MIN_NON_SHORT_LEN48;
408}
409
410#define MACSEC_NEEDED_HEADROOM(macsec_extra_len(true)) (macsec_extra_len(true))
411#define MACSEC_NEEDED_TAILROOM16 MACSEC_STD_ICV_LEN16
412
413static void macsec_fill_iv_xpn(unsigned char *iv, ssci_t ssci, u64 pn,
414 salt_t salt)
415{
416 struct gcm_iv_xpn *gcm_iv = (struct gcm_iv_xpn *)iv;
417
418 gcm_iv->ssci = ssci ^ salt.ssci;
419 gcm_iv->pn = cpu_to_be64(pn)(( __be64)(__u64)__builtin_bswap64((__u64)((pn)))) ^ salt.pn;
420}
421
422static void macsec_fill_iv(unsigned char *iv, sci_t sci, u32 pn)
423{
424 struct gcm_iv *gcm_iv = (struct gcm_iv *)iv;
425
426 gcm_iv->sci = sci;
427 gcm_iv->pn = htonl(pn)(( __be32)(__u32)__builtin_bswap32((__u32)((pn))));
428}
429
430static struct macsec_eth_header *macsec_ethhdr(struct sk_buff *skb)
431{
432 return (struct macsec_eth_header *)skb_mac_header(skb);
433}

443
444void macsec_pn_wrapped(struct macsec_secy *secy, struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa)
445{
446 spin_lock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
447 __macsec_pn_wrapped(secy, tx_sa);
448 spin_unlock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
449}
450EXPORT_SYMBOL_GPL(macsec_pn_wrapped)extern typeof(macsec_pn_wrapped) macsec_pn_wrapped; static void
* __attribute__((__used__)) __attribute__((__section__(".discard.addressable"
))) __UNIQUE_ID___addressable_macsec_pn_wrapped1954 = (void *
)(uintptr_t)&macsec_pn_wrapped; asm(".section \".export_symbol\",\"a\" ; __export_symbol_macsec_pn_wrapped: ; .asciz \"GPL\" ; .asciz \"\" ; .balign 8 ; .quad macsec_pn_wrapped ; .previous"
);
451
452static pn_t tx_sa_update_pn(struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa,
453 struct macsec_secy *secy)
454{
455 pn_t pn;
456
457 spin_lock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
458
459 pn = tx_sa->next_pn_halves;
460 if (secy->xpn)
461 tx_sa->next_pn++;
462 else
463 tx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower++;
464
465 if (tx_sa->next_pn == 0)
466 __macsec_pn_wrapped(secy, tx_sa);
467 spin_unlock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
468
469 return pn;
470}
471
472static void macsec_encrypt_finish(struct sk_buff *skb, struct net_device *dev)
473{
474 struct macsec_dev *macsec = netdev_priv(dev);
475
476 skb->dev = macsec->real_dev;
477 skb_reset_mac_header(skb);
478 skb->protocol = eth_hdr(skb)->h_proto;
479}
480
481static unsigned int macsec_msdu_len(struct sk_buff *skb)
482{
483 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(skb->dev);
484 struct macsec_secy *secy = &macsec->secy;
485 bool sci_present = macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci;
486
487 return skb->len - macsec_hdr_len(sci_present) - secy->icv_len;
488}

508
509static void count_tx(struct net_device *dev, int ret, int len)
510{
511 if (likely(ret == NET_XMIT_SUCCESS || ret == NET_XMIT_CN)__builtin_expect(!!(ret == 0x00 || ret == 0x02), 1))
512 dev_sw_netstats_tx_add(dev, 1, len);
513}
514
515static void macsec_encrypt_done(void *data, int err)
516{
517 struct sk_buff *skb = data;
518 struct net_device *dev = skb->dev;
519 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
520 struct macsec_tx_sa *sa = macsec_skb_cb(skb)->tx_sa;
521 int len, ret;
522
523 aead_request_free(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->req);
524
525 rcu_read_lock_bh();
526 macsec_count_tx(skb, &macsec->secy.tx_sc, macsec_skb_cb(skb)->tx_sa);
527 /* packet is encrypted/protected so tx_bytes must be calculated */
528 len = macsec_msdu_len(skb) + 2 * ETH_ALEN6;
529 macsec_encrypt_finish(skb, dev);
530 ret = dev_queue_xmit(skb);
531 count_tx(dev, ret, len);
532 rcu_read_unlock_bh();
533
534 macsec_txsa_put(sa);
535 dev_put(dev);
536}
537
538static struct aead_request *macsec_alloc_req(struct crypto_aead *tfm,
539 unsigned char **iv,
540 struct scatterlist **sg,
541 int num_frags)
542{
543 size_t size, iv_offset, sg_offset;
544 struct aead_request *req;
545 void *tmp;
546
547 size = sizeof(struct aead_request) + crypto_aead_reqsize(tfm);
548 iv_offset = size;
549 size += GCM_AES_IV_LEN12;
550
551 size = ALIGN(size, __alignof__(struct scatterlist))((((size)) + ((__typeof__((size)))((__alignof__(struct scatterlist
))) - 1)) & ~((__typeof__((size)))((__alignof__(struct scatterlist
))) - 1));
552 sg_offset = size;
553 size += sizeof(struct scatterlist) * num_frags;
554
555 tmp = kmalloc(size, GFP_ATOMIC)({ ; ({ struct alloc_tag * __attribute__((__unused__)) _old =
((void *)0); typeof(kmalloc_noprof(size, ((( gfp_t)((((1UL))
) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (
___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))))) _res = kmalloc_noprof(size, (
(( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)(((
(1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT))))); do {} while
(0); _res; }); });
556 if (!tmp)
557 return NULL((void *)0);
558
559 *iv = (unsigned char *)(tmp + iv_offset);
560 *sg = (struct scatterlist *)(tmp + sg_offset);
561 req = tmp;
562
563 aead_request_set_tfm(req, tfm);
564
565 return req;
566}
567
568static struct sk_buff *macsec_encrypt(struct sk_buff *skb,
569 struct net_device *dev)
570{
571 int ret;
572 struct scatterlist *sg;
573 struct sk_buff *trailer;
574 unsigned char *iv;
575 struct ethhdr *eth;
576 struct macsec_eth_header *hh;
577 size_t unprotected_len;
578 struct aead_request *req;
579 struct macsec_secy *secy;
580 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc;
581 struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa;
582 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
583 bool sci_present;
584 pn_t pn;
585
586 secy = &macsec->secy;
587 tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
588
589 /* 10.5.1 TX SA assignment */
590 tx_sa = macsec_txsa_get(tx_sc->sa[tx_sc->encoding_sa]);
591 if (!tx_sa) {
592 secy->operational = false;
593 kfree_skb(skb);
594 return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL22);
595 }
596
597 if (unlikely(skb_headroom(skb) < MACSEC_NEEDED_HEADROOM ||__builtin_expect(!!(skb_headroom(skb) < (macsec_extra_len(
true)) || skb_tailroom(skb) < 16), 0)
598 skb_tailroom(skb) < MACSEC_NEEDED_TAILROOM)__builtin_expect(!!(skb_headroom(skb) < (macsec_extra_len(
true)) || skb_tailroom(skb) < 16), 0)) {
599 struct sk_buff *nskb = skb_copy_expand(skb,
600 MACSEC_NEEDED_HEADROOM(macsec_extra_len(true)),
601 MACSEC_NEEDED_TAILROOM16,
602 GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
603 if (likely(nskb)__builtin_expect(!!(nskb), 1)) {
604 consume_skb(skb);
605 skb = nskb;
606 } else {
607 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
608 kfree_skb(skb);
609 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
610 }
611 } else {
612 skb = skb_unshare(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
613 if (!skb) {
614 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
615 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
616 }
617 }
618
619 unprotected_len = skb->len;
620 eth = eth_hdr(skb);
621 sci_present = macsec_send_sci(secy);
622 hh = skb_push(skb, macsec_extra_len(sci_present));
623 memmove(hh, eth, 2 * ETH_ALEN)({ const size_t __fortify_size = (size_t)(2 * 6); const size_t
__p_size = (__builtin_dynamic_object_size(hh, 0)); const size_t
__q_size = (__builtin_dynamic_object_size(eth, 0)); const size_t
__p_size_field = (__builtin_dynamic_object_size(hh, 1)); const
size_t __q_size_field = (__builtin_dynamic_object_size(eth, 1
)); ({ bool __ret_do_once = !!(fortify_memcpy_chk(__fortify_size
, __p_size, __q_size, __p_size_field, __q_size_field, FORTIFY_FUNC_memmove
)); if (({ static bool __attribute__((__section__(".data.once"
))) __already_done; bool __ret_cond = !!(__ret_do_once); bool
__ret_once = false; if (__builtin_expect(!!(__ret_cond &&
!__already_done), 0)) { __already_done = true; __ret_once = true
; } __builtin_expect(!!(__ret_once), 0); })) ({ int __ret_warn_on
= !!(1); if (__builtin_expect(!!(__ret_warn_on), 0)) do { ({
asm volatile("1955" ": nop\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.instr_begin\n\t"
".long " "1955" "b - .\n\t" ".popsection\n\t" : : "i" (1955)
); }); __warn_printk("memmove" ": detected field-spanning write (size %zu) of single %s (size %zu)\n"
, __fortify_size, "field \"" "hh" "\" at " "drivers/net/macsec.c"
":" "623", __p_size_field); do { __auto_type __flags = (1 <<

": nop\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.instr_begin\n\t" ".long "

{ asm __inline volatile("1:\t" ".byte 0x0f, 0x0b" "\n" ".pushsection __bug_table,\"aw\"\n"

".popsection\n\t" : : "i" ("drivers/net/macsec.c"), "i" (623
), "i" (__flags), "i" (sizeof(struct bug_entry))); } while (0
); ({ asm volatile("1957" ": nop\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.instr_end\n\t"

".long " "1958" "b - .\n\t" ".popsection\n\t" : : "i" (1958)
); }); } while (0); __builtin_expect(!!(__ret_warn_on), 0); }
); __builtin_expect(!!(__ret_do_once), 0); }); __builtin_memmove

624
625 pn = tx_sa_update_pn(tx_sa, secy);
626 if (pn.full64 == 0) {
627 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
628 kfree_skb(skb);
629 return ERR_PTR(-ENOLINK67);
630 }
631 macsec_fill_sectag(hh, secy, pn.lower, sci_present);
632 macsec_set_shortlen(hh, unprotected_len - 2 * ETH_ALEN6);
633
634 skb_put(skb, secy->icv_len);
635
636 if (skb->len - ETH_HLEN14 > macsec_priv(dev)->real_dev->mtu) {
637 struct pcpu_secy_stats *secy_stats = this_cpu_ptr(macsec->stats)({ do { const void *__vpp_verify = (typeof((macsec->stats)
+ 0))((void *)0); (void)__vpp_verify; } while (0); ({ unsigned
long __ptr; __ptr = (unsigned long) ((typeof(*(macsec->stats
)) *)(macsec->stats)); (typeof((typeof(*(macsec->stats)
) *)(macsec->stats))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset[debug_smp_processor_id
()])))); }); });
638
639 u64_stats_update_begin(&secy_stats->syncp);
640 secy_stats->stats.OutPktsTooLong++;
641 u64_stats_update_end(&secy_stats->syncp);
642
643 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
644 kfree_skb(skb);
645 return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL22);
646 }
647
648 ret = skb_cow_data(skb, 0, &trailer);
649 if (unlikely(ret < 0)__builtin_expect(!!(ret < 0), 0)) {
650 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
651 kfree_skb(skb);
652 return ERR_PTR(ret);
653 }
654
655 req = macsec_alloc_req(tx_sa->key.tfm, &iv, &sg, ret);
656 if (!req) {
657 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
658 kfree_skb(skb);
659 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
660 }
661
662 if (secy->xpn)
663 macsec_fill_iv_xpn(iv, tx_sa->ssci, pn.full64, tx_sa->key.salt);
664 else
665 macsec_fill_iv(iv, secy->sci, pn.lower);
666
667 sg_init_table(sg, ret);
668 ret = skb_to_sgvec(skb, sg, 0, skb->len);
669 if (unlikely(ret < 0)__builtin_expect(!!(ret < 0), 0)) {
670 aead_request_free(req);
671 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
672 kfree_skb(skb);
673 return ERR_PTR(ret);
674 }
675
676 if (tx_sc->encrypt) {
677 int len = skb->len - macsec_hdr_len(sci_present) -
678 secy->icv_len;
679 aead_request_set_crypt(req, sg, sg, len, iv);
680 aead_request_set_ad(req, macsec_hdr_len(sci_present));
681 } else {
682 aead_request_set_crypt(req, sg, sg, 0, iv);
683 aead_request_set_ad(req, skb->len - secy->icv_len);
684 }
685
686 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->req = req;
687 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->tx_sa = tx_sa;
688 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci = sci_present;
689 aead_request_set_callback(req, 0, macsec_encrypt_done, skb);
690
691 dev_hold(skb->dev);
692 ret = crypto_aead_encrypt(req);
693 if (ret == -EINPROGRESS115) {
694 return ERR_PTR(ret);
695 } else if (ret != 0) {
696 dev_put(skb->dev);
697 kfree_skb(skb);
698 aead_request_free(req);
699 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
700 return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL22);
701 }
702
703 dev_put(skb->dev);
704 aead_request_free(req);
705 macsec_txsa_put(tx_sa);
706
707 return skb;
708}

710static bool macsec_post_decrypt(struct sk_buff *skb, struct macsec_secy *secy, u32 pn)
711{

sc->stats)) *)(rx_sa->sc->stats))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset
[debug_smp_processor_id()])))); }); });

722 * (see IEEE 802.1AE-2006 figure 10-5)

785 }

788 }

791}
792
793static void macsec_reset_skb(struct sk_buff *skb, struct net_device *dev)
794{
795 skb->pkt_type = PACKET_HOST0;
796 skb->protocol = eth_type_trans(skb, dev);
797
798 skb_reset_network_header(skb);
799 if (!skb_transport_header_was_set(skb))
800 skb_reset_transport_header(skb);
801 skb_reset_mac_len(skb);
802}
803

812static void count_rx(struct net_device *dev, int len)
813{
814 dev_sw_netstats_rx_add(dev, len);
815}
816
817static void macsec_decrypt_done(void *data, int err)
818{
819 struct sk_buff *skb = data;
820 struct net_device *dev = skb->dev;
821 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
822 struct macsec_rx_sa *rx_sa = macsec_skb_cb(skb)->rx_sa;
823 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc = rx_sa->sc;
824 int len;
825 u32 pn;
826
827 aead_request_free(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->req);
828
829 if (!err)
830 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->valid = true;
831
832 rcu_read_lock_bh();
833 pn = ntohl(macsec_ethhdr(skb)->packet_number)(__u32)__builtin_bswap32((__u32)(( __u32)(__be32)(macsec_ethhdr
(skb)->packet_number)));
834 if (!macsec_post_decrypt(skb, &macsec->secy, pn)) {
835 rcu_read_unlock_bh();
836 kfree_skb(skb);
837 goto out;
838 }
839
840 macsec_finalize_skb(skb, macsec->secy.icv_len,
841 macsec_extra_len(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci));
842 len = skb->len;
843 macsec_reset_skb(skb, macsec->secy.netdev);
844
845 if (gro_cells_receive(&macsec->gro_cells, skb) == NET_RX_SUCCESS0)
846 count_rx(dev, len);
847
848 rcu_read_unlock_bh();
849
850out:
851 macsec_rxsa_put(rx_sa);
852 macsec_rxsc_put(rx_sc);
853 dev_put(dev);
854}
855
856static struct sk_buff *macsec_decrypt(struct sk_buff *skb,
857 struct net_device *dev,
858 struct macsec_rx_sa *rx_sa,
859 sci_t sci,
860 struct macsec_secy *secy)
861{
862 int ret;
863 struct scatterlist *sg;
864 struct sk_buff *trailer;
865 unsigned char *iv;
866 struct aead_request *req;
867 struct macsec_eth_header *hdr;
868 u32 hdr_pn;
869 u16 icv_len = secy->icv_len;
870
871 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->valid = false;
872 skb = skb_share_check(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
873 if (!skb)
874 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
875
876 ret = skb_cow_data(skb, 0, &trailer);
877 if (unlikely(ret < 0)__builtin_expect(!!(ret < 0), 0)) {
878 kfree_skb(skb);
879 return ERR_PTR(ret);
880 }
881 req = macsec_alloc_req(rx_sa->key.tfm, &iv, &sg, ret);
882 if (!req) {
883 kfree_skb(skb);
884 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
885 }
886
887 hdr = (struct macsec_eth_header *)skb->data;
888 hdr_pn = ntohl(hdr->packet_number)(__u32)__builtin_bswap32((__u32)(( __u32)(__be32)(hdr->packet_number
)));
889
890 if (secy->xpn) {
891 pn_t recovered_pn = rx_sa->next_pn_halves;
892
893 recovered_pn.lower = hdr_pn;
894 if (hdr_pn < rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower &&
895 !pn_same_half(hdr_pn, rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower)(!(((hdr_pn) >> 31) ^ ((rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower)
>> 31))))
896 recovered_pn.upper++;
897
898 macsec_fill_iv_xpn(iv, rx_sa->ssci, recovered_pn.full64,
899 rx_sa->key.salt);
900 } else {
901 macsec_fill_iv(iv, sci, hdr_pn);
902 }
903
904 sg_init_table(sg, ret);
905 ret = skb_to_sgvec(skb, sg, 0, skb->len);
906 if (unlikely(ret < 0)__builtin_expect(!!(ret < 0), 0)) {
907 aead_request_free(req);
908 kfree_skb(skb);
909 return ERR_PTR(ret);
910 }
911
912 if (hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_E0x08) {
913 /* confidentiality: ethernet + macsec header
914 * authenticated, encrypted payload
915 */
916 int len = skb->len - macsec_hdr_len(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci);
917
918 aead_request_set_crypt(req, sg, sg, len, iv);
919 aead_request_set_ad(req, macsec_hdr_len(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci));
920 skb = skb_unshare(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
921 if (!skb) {
922 aead_request_free(req);
923 return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM12);
924 }
925 } else {
926 /* integrity only: all headers + data authenticated */
927 aead_request_set_crypt(req, sg, sg, icv_len, iv);
928 aead_request_set_ad(req, skb->len - icv_len);
929 }
930
931 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->req = req;
932 skb->dev = dev;
933 aead_request_set_callback(req, 0, macsec_decrypt_done, skb);
934
935 dev_hold(dev);
936 ret = crypto_aead_decrypt(req);
937 if (ret == -EINPROGRESS115) {
938 return ERR_PTR(ret);
939 } else if (ret != 0) {
940 /* decryption/authentication failed
941 * 10.6 if validateFrames is disabled, deliver anyway
942 */
943 if (ret != -EBADMSG74) {
944 kfree_skb(skb);
945 skb = ERR_PTR(ret);
946 }
947 } else {
948 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->valid = true;
949 }
950 dev_put(dev);
951
952 aead_request_free(req);
953
954 return skb;
955}

); ((typeof(*macsec) *)(__mptr - __builtin_offsetof(typeof(*macsec

997 struct sk_buff *nskb;
998 struct pcpu_secy_stats *secy_stats = this_cpu_ptr(macsec->stats)({ do { const void *__vpp_verify = (typeof((macsec->stats)
+ 0))((void *)0); (void)__vpp_verify; } while (0); ({ unsigned
long __ptr; __ptr = (unsigned long) ((typeof(*(macsec->stats
)) *)(macsec->stats)); (typeof((typeof(*(macsec->stats)
) *)(macsec->stats))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset[debug_smp_processor_id
()])))); }); });
999 struct net_device *ndev = macsec->secy.netdev;
1000
1001 /* If h/w offloading is enabled, HW decodes frames and strips
1002 * the SecTAG, so we have to deduce which port to deliver to.
1003 */
1004 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec) && netif_running(ndev)) {
1005 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
1006
1007 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, NULL((void *)0));
1008
1009 if (ops->rx_uses_md_dst && !is_macsec_md_dst)
1010 continue;
1011
1012 if (is_macsec_md_dst) {
1013 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc;
1014
1015 /* All drivers that implement MACsec offload
1016 * support using skb metadata destinations must
1017 * indicate that they do so.
1018 */
1019 DEBUG_NET_WARN_ON_ONCE(!ops->rx_uses_md_dst)(void)({ int __ret_warn_on = !!(!ops->rx_uses_md_dst); if (
__builtin_expect(!!(__ret_warn_on), 0)) do { __auto_type __flags
= (1 << 0)|((1 << 1) | ((9) << 8)); ({ asm
volatile("1969" ": nop\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.instr_begin\n\t"
".long " "1969" "b - .\n\t" ".popsection\n\t" : : "i" (1969)
); }); do { asm __inline volatile("1:\t" ".byte 0x0f, 0x0b" "\n"
".pushsection __bug_table,\"aw\"\n" "2:\t" ".long " "1b" " - ."
"\t# bug_entry::bug_addr\n" "\t" ".long " "%c0" " - ." "\t# bug_entry::file\n"
"\t.word %c1" "\t# bug_entry::line\n" "\t.word %c2" "\t# bug_entry::flags\n"
"\t.org 2b+%c3\n" ".popsection\n" "998:\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.reachable\n\t"
".long 998b\n\t" ".popsection\n\t" : : "i" ("drivers/net/macsec.c"
), "i" (1019), "i" (__flags), "i" (sizeof(struct bug_entry)))
; } while (0); ({ asm volatile("1970" ": nop\n\t" ".pushsection .discard.instr_end\n\t"
".long " "1970" "b - .\n\t" ".popsection\n\t" : : "i" (1970)
); }); } while (0); __builtin_expect(!!(__ret_warn_on), 0); }
);
1020 rx_sc = find_rx_sc(&macsec->secy,
1021 md_dst->u.macsec_info.sci);
1022 if (!rx_sc)
1023 continue;
1024 /* device indicated macsec offload occurred */
1025 skb->dev = ndev;
1026 skb->pkt_type = PACKET_HOST0;
1027 eth_skb_pkt_type(skb, ndev);
1028 ret = RX_HANDLER_ANOTHER;
1029 goto out;
1030 }
1031
1032 /* This datapath is insecure because it is unable to
1033 * enforce isolation of broadcast/multicast traffic and
1034 * unicast traffic with promiscuous mode on the macsec
1035 * netdev. Since the core stack has no mechanism to
1036 * check that the hardware did indeed receive MACsec
1037 * traffic, it is possible that the response handling
1038 * done by the MACsec port was to a plaintext packet.
1039 * This violates the MACsec protocol standard.
1040 */
1041 if (ether_addr_equal_64bits(hdr->h_dest,
1042 ndev->dev_addr)) {
1043 /* exact match, divert skb to this port */
1044 skb->dev = ndev;
1045 skb->pkt_type = PACKET_HOST0;
1046 ret = RX_HANDLER_ANOTHER;
1047 goto out;
1048 } else if (is_multicast_ether_addr_64bits(
1049 hdr->h_dest)) {
1050 /* multicast frame, deliver on this port too */
1051 nskb = skb_clone(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
1052 if (!nskb)
1053 break;
1054
1055 nskb->dev = ndev;
1056 eth_skb_pkt_type(nskb, ndev);
1057
1058 __netif_rx(nskb);
1059 } else if (ndev->flags & IFF_PROMISCIFF_PROMISC) {
1060 skb->dev = ndev;
1061 skb->pkt_type = PACKET_HOST0;
1062 ret = RX_HANDLER_ANOTHER;
1063 goto out;
1064 }
1065
1066 continue;
1067 }
1068
1069 /* 10.6 If the management control validateFrames is not
1070 * Strict, frames without a SecTAG are received, counted, and
1071 * delivered to the Controlled Port
1072 */
1073 if (macsec->secy.validate_frames == MACSEC_VALIDATE_STRICT) {
1074 u64_stats_update_begin(&secy_stats->syncp);
1075 secy_stats->stats.InPktsNoTag++;
1076 u64_stats_update_end(&secy_stats->syncp);
1077 DEV_STATS_INC(macsec->secy.netdev, rx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(macsec->secy.netdev)->stats.__rx_dropped
);
1078 continue;
1079 }
1080
1081 /* deliver on this port */
1082 nskb = skb_clone(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
1083 if (!nskb)
1084 break;
1085
1086 nskb->dev = ndev;
1087
1088 if (__netif_rx(nskb) == NET_RX_SUCCESS0) {
1089 u64_stats_update_begin(&secy_stats->syncp);
1090 secy_stats->stats.InPktsUntagged++;
1091 u64_stats_update_end(&secy_stats->syncp);
1092 }
1093 }
1094
1095out:
1096 rcu_read_unlock();
1097 return ret;
1098}
1099
1100static rx_handler_result_t macsec_handle_frame(struct sk_buff **pskb)
1101{
1102 struct sk_buff *skb = *pskb;
1103 struct net_device *dev = skb->dev;
1104 struct macsec_eth_header *hdr;
1105 struct macsec_secy *secy = NULL((void *)0);
1106 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc;
1107 struct macsec_rx_sa *rx_sa;
1108 struct macsec_rxh_data *rxd;
1109 struct macsec_dev *macsec;
1110 unsigned int len;
1111 sci_t sci;
1112 u32 hdr_pn;
1113 bool cbit;
1114 struct pcpu_rx_sc_stats *rxsc_stats;
1115 struct pcpu_secy_stats *secy_stats;
1116 bool pulled_sci;
1117 int ret;
1118
1119 if (skb_headroom(skb) < ETH_HLEN14)
1120 goto drop_direct;
1121
1122 hdr = macsec_ethhdr(skb);
1123 if (hdr->eth.h_proto != htons(ETH_P_MACSEC)(( __be16)(__u16)__builtin_bswap16((__u16)((0x88E5)))))
1124 return handle_not_macsec(skb);
1125
1126 skb = skb_unshare(skb, GFP_ATOMIC((( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_HIGH_BIT)))|(( gfp_t)((
((1UL))) << (___GFP_KSWAPD_RECLAIM_BIT)))));
1127 *pskb = skb;
1128 if (!skb)
1129 return RX_HANDLER_CONSUMED;
1130
1131 pulled_sci = pskb_may_pull(skb, macsec_extra_len(true));
1132 if (!pulled_sci) {
1133 if (!pskb_may_pull(skb, macsec_extra_len(false)))
1134 goto drop_direct;
1135 }
1136
1137 hdr = macsec_ethhdr(skb);
1138
1139 /* Frames with a SecTAG that has the TCI E bit set but the C
1140 * bit clear are discarded, as this reserved encoding is used
1141 * to identify frames with a SecTAG that are not to be
1142 * delivered to the Controlled Port.
1143 */
1144 if ((hdr->tci_an & (MACSEC_TCI_C0x04 | MACSEC_TCI_E0x08)) == MACSEC_TCI_E0x08)
1145 return RX_HANDLER_PASS;
1146
1147 /* now, pull the extra length */
1148 if (hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_SC0x20) {
1149 if (!pulled_sci)
1150 goto drop_direct;
1151 }
1152
1153 /* ethernet header is part of crypto processing */
1154 skb_push(skb, ETH_HLEN14);
1155
1156 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci = !!(hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_SC0x20);
1157 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->assoc_num = hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_AN_MASK0x03;
1158 sci = macsec_frame_sci(hdr, macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci);
1159
1160 rcu_read_lock();
1161 rxd = macsec_data_rcu(skb->dev);
1162

1165
1166 sc = sc ? macsec_rxsc_get(sc) : NULL((void *)0);
1167
1168 if (sc) {
1169 secy = &macsec->secy;
1170 rx_sc = sc;
1171 break;
1172 }
1173 }
1174
1175 if (!secy)
1176 goto nosci;
1177
1178 dev = secy->netdev;

1180 secy_stats = this_cpu_ptr(macsec->stats)({ do { const void *__vpp_verify = (typeof((macsec->stats)
+ 0))((void *)0); (void)__vpp_verify; } while (0); ({ unsigned

long __ptr; __ptr = (unsigned long) ((typeof(*(rx_sc->stats
)) *)(rx_sc->stats)); (typeof((typeof(*(rx_sc->stats)) *
)(rx_sc->stats))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset[debug_smp_processor_id

1189 }

1196 * SecTAG is set, discard
1197 */

1199 secy->validate_frames == MACSEC_VALIDATE_STRICT) {

1201 rxsc_stats->stats.InPktsNotUsingSA++;
1202 u64_stats_update_end(&rxsc_stats->syncp);

1204 goto drop_nosa;
1205 }
1206
1207 /* not Strict, the frame (with the SecTAG and ICV
1208 * removed) is delivered to the Controlled Port.
1209 */
1210 u64_stats_update_begin(&rxsc_stats->syncp);
1211 rxsc_stats->stats.InPktsUnusedSA++;
1212 u64_stats_update_end(&rxsc_stats->syncp);
1213 goto deliver;
1214 }
1215
1216 /* First, PN check to avoid decrypting obviously wrong packets */
1217 hdr_pn = ntohl(hdr->packet_number)(__u32)__builtin_bswap32((__u32)(( __u32)(__be32)(hdr->packet_number
)));
1218 if (secy->replay_protect) {
1219 bool late;
1220
1221 spin_lock(&rx_sa->lock);
1222 late = rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower >= secy->replay_window &&
1223 hdr_pn < (rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower - secy->replay_window);
1224
1225 if (secy->xpn)
1226 late = late && pn_same_half(rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower, hdr_pn)(!(((rx_sa->next_pn_halves.lower) >> 31) ^ ((hdr_pn)
>> 31)));
1227 spin_unlock(&rx_sa->lock);
1228
1229 if (late) {
1230 u64_stats_update_begin(&rxsc_stats->syncp);
1231 rxsc_stats->stats.InPktsLate++;
1232 u64_stats_update_end(&rxsc_stats->syncp);
1233 DEV_STATS_INC(macsec->secy.netdev, rx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(macsec->secy.netdev)->stats.__rx_dropped
);
1234 goto drop;
1235 }
1236 }
1237
1238 macsec_skb_cb(skb)->rx_sa = rx_sa;
1239
1240 /* Disabled && !changed text => skip validation */
1241 if (hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_C0x04 ||
1242 secy->validate_frames != MACSEC_VALIDATE_DISABLED)
1243 skb = macsec_decrypt(skb, dev, rx_sa, sci, secy);
1244
1245 if (IS_ERR(skb)) {
1246 /* the decrypt callback needs the reference */
1247 if (PTR_ERR(skb) != -EINPROGRESS115) {
1248 macsec_rxsa_put(rx_sa);
1249 macsec_rxsc_put(rx_sc);
1250 }
1251 rcu_read_unlock();
1252 *pskb = NULL((void *)0);
1253 return RX_HANDLER_CONSUMED;
1254 }
1255
1256 if (!macsec_post_decrypt(skb, secy, hdr_pn))
1257 goto drop;
1258
1259deliver:
1260 macsec_finalize_skb(skb, secy->icv_len,
1261 macsec_extra_len(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci));
1262 len = skb->len;
1263 macsec_reset_skb(skb, secy->netdev);
1264
1265 if (rx_sa)
1266 macsec_rxsa_put(rx_sa);
1267 macsec_rxsc_put(rx_sc);
1268
1269 skb_orphan(skb);
1270 ret = gro_cells_receive(&macsec->gro_cells, skb);
1271 if (ret == NET_RX_SUCCESS0)
1272 count_rx(dev, len);
1273 else
1274 DEV_STATS_INC(macsec->secy.netdev, rx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(macsec->secy.netdev)->stats.__rx_dropped
);
1275
1276 rcu_read_unlock();
1277
1278 *pskb = NULL((void *)0);
1279 return RX_HANDLER_CONSUMED;
1280
1281drop:
1282 macsec_rxsa_put(rx_sa);
1283drop_nosa:
1284 macsec_rxsc_put(rx_sc);
1285 rcu_read_unlock();
1286drop_direct:
1287 kfree_skb(skb);
1288 *pskb = NULL((void *)0);
1289 return RX_HANDLER_CONSUMED;
1290
1291nosci:
1292 /* 10.6.1 if the SC is not found */
1293 cbit = !!(hdr->tci_an & MACSEC_TCI_C0x04);
1294 if (!cbit)
1295 macsec_finalize_skb(skb, MACSEC_DEFAULT_ICV_LEN16,
1296 macsec_extra_len(macsec_skb_cb(skb)->has_sci));
1297

1339static struct crypto_aead *macsec_alloc_tfm(char *key, int key_len, int icv_len)
1340{
1341 struct crypto_aead *tfm;
1342 int ret;
1343
1344 tfm = crypto_alloc_aead("gcm(aes)", 0, 0);
1345
1346 if (IS_ERR(tfm))
1347 return tfm;
1348
1349 ret = crypto_aead_setkey(tfm, key, key_len);
1350 if (ret < 0)
1351 goto fail;
1352
1353 ret = crypto_aead_setauthsize(tfm, icv_len);
1354 if (ret < 0)
1355 goto fail;
1356
1357 return tfm;
1358fail:
1359 crypto_free_aead(tfm);
1360 return ERR_PTR(ret);
1361}

1493static struct net_device *get_dev_from_nl(struct net *net,
1494 struct nlattr **attrs)
1495{
1496 int ifindex = nla_get_u32(attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX]);
1497 struct net_device *dev;
1498
1499 dev = __dev_get_by_index(net, ifindex);
1500 if (!dev)
1501 return ERR_PTR(-ENODEV19);
1502
1503 if (!netif_is_macsec(dev))
1504 return ERR_PTR(-ENODEV19);
1505
1506 return dev;
1507}

1535static struct macsec_tx_sa *get_txsa_from_nl(struct net *net,
1536 struct nlattr **attrs,
1537 struct nlattr **tb_sa,
1538 struct net_device **devp,
1539 struct macsec_secy **secyp,
1540 struct macsec_tx_sc **scp,
1541 u8 *assoc_num)
1542{
1543 struct net_device *dev;
1544 struct macsec_secy *secy;
1545 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc;
1546 struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa;
1547
1548 if (!tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN])
1549 return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL22);
1550
1551 *assoc_num = nla_get_u8(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN]);
1552
1553 dev = get_dev_from_nl(net, attrs);
1554 if (IS_ERR(dev))
1555 return ERR_CAST(dev);
1556
1557 if (*assoc_num >= MACSEC_NUM_AN4)
1558 return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL22);
1559
1560 secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
1561 tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
1562
1563 tx_sa = rtnl_dereference(tx_sc->sa[*assoc_num])({ do { static bool __attribute__((__section__(".data.unlikely"
))) __warned; if (debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled() && (!((
lockdep_rtnl_is_held()))) && debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled
() && !__warned) { __warned = true; lockdep_rcu_suspicious
("drivers/net/macsec.c", 1563, "suspicious rcu_dereference_protected() usage"
); } } while (0); ; ((typeof(*(tx_sc->sa[*assoc_num])) *)(
(tx_sc->sa[*assoc_num]))); });
1564 if (!tx_sa)
1565 return ERR_PTR(-ENODEV19);
1566
1567 *devp = dev;
1568 *scp = tx_sc;
1569 *secyp = secy;
1570 return tx_sa;
1571}

1664/* Offloads an operation to a device driver */
1665static int macsec_offload(int (* const func)(struct macsec_context *),
1666 struct macsec_context *ctx)
1667{
1668 int ret;
1669
1670 if (unlikely(!func)__builtin_expect(!!(!func), 0))
1671 return 0;
1672
1673 if (ctx->offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
1674 mutex_lock(&ctx->phydev->lock)mutex_lock_nested(&ctx->phydev->lock, 0);
1675
1676 ret = (*func)(ctx);
1677
1678 if (ctx->offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY)
1679 mutex_unlock(&ctx->phydev->lock);
1680
1681 return ret;
1682}

1684static int parse_sa_config(struct nlattr **attrs, struct nlattr **tb_sa)
1685{
1686 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_SA_CONFIG])
1687 return -EINVAL22;
1688
1689 if (nla_parse_nested_deprecated(tb_sa, MACSEC_SA_ATTR_MAX, attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_SA_CONFIG], macsec_genl_sa_policy, NULL((void *)0)))
1690 return -EINVAL22;
1691
1692 return 0;
1693}

1695static int parse_rxsc_config(struct nlattr **attrs, struct nlattr **tb_rxsc)
1696{
1697 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_RXSC_CONFIG])
1698 return -EINVAL22;
1699
1700 if (nla_parse_nested_deprecated(tb_rxsc, MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_MAX, attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_RXSC_CONFIG], macsec_genl_rxsc_policy, NULL((void *)0)))
1701 return -EINVAL22;
1702
1703 return 0;
1704}
1705
1706static bool validate_add_rxsa(struct nlattr **attrs)
1707{
1708 if (!attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN] ||
1709 !attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEY] ||
1710 !attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEYID])
1711 return false;
1712
1713 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN]) >= MACSEC_NUM_AN4)
1714 return false;
1715
1716 if (attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN] &&
1717 nla_get_u64(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) == 0)
1718 return false;
1719
1720 if (attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) {
1721 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) > 1)
1722 return false;
1723 }
1724
1725 if (nla_len(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEYID]) != MACSEC_KEYID_LEN16)
1726 return false;
1727
1728 return true;
1729}

1868
1869static bool validate_add_rxsc(struct nlattr **attrs)
1870{
1871 if (!attrs[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_SCI])
1872 return false;
1873
1874 if (attrs[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE]) {
1875 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE]) > 1)
1876 return false;
1877 }
1878
1879 return true;
1880}
1881
1882static int macsec_add_rxsc(struct sk_buff *skb, struct genl_info *info)
1883{
1884 struct net_device *dev;
1885 sci_t sci = MACSEC_UNDEF_SCI(( sci_t)0xffffffffffffffffULL);
1886 struct nlattr **attrs = info->attrs;
1887 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc;
1888 struct nlattr *tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_MAX + 1];
1889 struct macsec_secy *secy;
1890 bool active = true;
1891 int ret;
1892
1893 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX])
1894 return -EINVAL22;
1895
1896 if (parse_rxsc_config(attrs, tb_rxsc))
1897 return -EINVAL22;
1898
1899 if (!validate_add_rxsc(tb_rxsc))
1900 return -EINVAL22;
1901
1902 rtnl_lock();
1903 dev = get_dev_from_nl(genl_info_net(info), attrs);
1904 if (IS_ERR(dev)) {
1905 rtnl_unlock();
1906 return PTR_ERR(dev);
1907 }
1908
1909 secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
1910 sci = nla_get_sci(tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_SCI]);
1911
1912 if (tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE])
1913 active = nla_get_u8(tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE]);
1914
1915 rx_sc = create_rx_sc(dev, sci, active);
1916 if (IS_ERR(rx_sc)) {
1917 rtnl_unlock();
1918 return PTR_ERR(rx_sc);
1919 }
1920
1921 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
1922 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
1923 struct macsec_context ctx;
1924
1925 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
1926 if (!ops) {
1927 ret = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
1928 goto cleanup;
1929 }
1930
1931 ctx.rx_sc = rx_sc;
1932 ctx.secy = secy;
1933
1934 ret = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_add_rxsc, &ctx);
1935 if (ret)
1936 goto cleanup;
1937 }
1938
1939 rtnl_unlock();
1940
1941 return 0;
1942
1943cleanup:
1944 del_rx_sc(secy, sci);
1945 free_rx_sc(rx_sc);
1946 rtnl_unlock();
1947 return ret;
1948}
1949
1950static bool validate_add_txsa(struct nlattr **attrs)
1951{
1952 if (!attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN] ||
1953 !attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN] ||
1954 !attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEY] ||
1955 !attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEYID])
1956 return false;
1957
1958 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN]) >= MACSEC_NUM_AN4)
1959 return false;
1960
1961 if (nla_get_u64(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) == 0)
1962 return false;
1963
1964 if (attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) {
1965 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) > 1)
1966 return false;
1967 }
1968
1969 if (nla_len(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEYID]) != MACSEC_KEYID_LEN16)
1970 return false;
1971
1972 return true;
1973}

2179static int macsec_del_rxsc(struct sk_buff *skb, struct genl_info *info)
2180{
2181 struct nlattr **attrs = info->attrs;
2182 struct net_device *dev;
2183 struct macsec_secy *secy;
2184 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc;
2185 sci_t sci;
2186 struct nlattr *tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_MAX + 1];
2187 int ret;
2188
2189 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX])
2190 return -EINVAL22;
2191
2192 if (parse_rxsc_config(attrs, tb_rxsc))
2193 return -EINVAL22;
2194
2195 if (!tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_SCI])
2196 return -EINVAL22;
2197
2198 rtnl_lock();
2199 dev = get_dev_from_nl(genl_info_net(info), info->attrs);
2200 if (IS_ERR(dev)) {
2201 rtnl_unlock();
2202 return PTR_ERR(dev);
2203 }
2204
2205 secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
2206 sci = nla_get_sci(tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_SCI]);
2207
2208 rx_sc = del_rx_sc(secy, sci);
2209 if (!rx_sc) {
2210 rtnl_unlock();
2211 return -ENODEV19;
2212 }
2213
2214 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
2215 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
2216 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2217 struct macsec_context ctx;
2218
2219 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
2220 if (!ops) {
2221 ret = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2222 goto cleanup;
2223 }
2224
2225 ctx.rx_sc = rx_sc;
2226 ctx.secy = secy;
2227 ret = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_del_rxsc, &ctx);
2228 if (ret)
2229 goto cleanup;
2230 }
2231
2232 free_rx_sc(rx_sc);
2233 rtnl_unlock();
2234
2235 return 0;
2236
2237cleanup:
2238 rtnl_unlock();
2239 return ret;

2273 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
2274 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2275 struct macsec_context ctx;
2276
2277 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
2278 if (!ops) {
2279 ret = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2280 goto cleanup;
2281 }
2282
2283 ctx.sa.assoc_num = assoc_num;
2284 ctx.sa.tx_sa = tx_sa;
2285 ctx.secy = secy;
2286
2287 ret = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_del_txsa, &ctx);
2288 if (ret)
2289 goto cleanup;
2290 }
2291
2292 RCU_INIT_POINTER(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num], NULL)do { ; do { do { __attribute__((__noreturn__)) extern void __compiletime_assert_2000
(void) __attribute__((__error__("Unsupported access size for {READ,WRITE}_ONCE()."
))); if (!((sizeof(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num]) == sizeof(char) ||
sizeof(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num]) == sizeof(short) || sizeof(tx_sc
->sa[assoc_num]) == sizeof(int) || sizeof(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num
]) == sizeof(long)) || sizeof(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num]) == sizeof
(long long))) __compiletime_assert_2000(); } while (0); do { *
(volatile typeof(tx_sc->sa[assoc_num]) *)&(tx_sc->sa
[assoc_num]) = ((typeof(*(((void *)0))) *)(((void *)0))); } while
(0); } while (0); } while (0);
2293 clear_tx_sa(tx_sa);
2294
2295 rtnl_unlock();
2296
2297 return 0;
2298
2299cleanup:
2300 rtnl_unlock();
2301 return ret;
2302}
2303
2304static bool validate_upd_sa(struct nlattr **attrs)
2305{
2306 if (!attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN] ||
2307 attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEY] ||
2308 attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_KEYID] ||
2309 attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_SSCI] ||
2310 attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_SALT])
2311 return false;
2312
2313 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_AN]) >= MACSEC_NUM_AN4)
2314 return false;
2315
2316 if (attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN] && nla_get_u64(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) == 0)
2317 return false;
2318
2319 if (attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) {
2320 if (nla_get_u8(attrs[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]) > 1)
2321 return false;
2322 }
2323
2324 return true;
2325}
2326
2327static int macsec_upd_txsa(struct sk_buff *skb, struct genl_info *info)
2328{
2329 struct nlattr **attrs = info->attrs;
2330 struct net_device *dev;
2331 struct macsec_secy *secy;
2332 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc;
2333 struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa;
2334 u8 assoc_num;
2335 struct nlattr *tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_MAX + 1];
2336 bool was_operational, was_active;
2337 pn_t prev_pn;
2338 int ret = 0;
2339
2340 prev_pn.full64 = 0;
2341
2342 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX])
2343 return -EINVAL22;
2344
2345 if (parse_sa_config(attrs, tb_sa))
2346 return -EINVAL22;
2347
2348 if (!validate_upd_sa(tb_sa))
2349 return -EINVAL22;
2350
2351 rtnl_lock();
2352 tx_sa = get_txsa_from_nl(genl_info_net(info), attrs, tb_sa,
2353 &dev, &secy, &tx_sc, &assoc_num);
2354 if (IS_ERR(tx_sa)) {
2355 rtnl_unlock();
2356 return PTR_ERR(tx_sa);
2357 }
2358
2359 if (tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) {
2360 int pn_len;
2361
2362 pn_len = secy->xpn ? MACSEC_XPN_PN_LEN8 : MACSEC_DEFAULT_PN_LEN4;
2363 if (nla_len(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) != pn_len) {
2364 pr_notice("macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n",({ do { if (__builtin_constant_p("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) && __builtin_constant_p(((void *)0))) { static const
struct pi_entry _entry __attribute__((__used__)) = { .fmt = __builtin_constant_p
("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) ? ("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) : ((void *)0), .func = __func__, .file = "drivers/net/macsec.c"
, .line = 2365, .level = __builtin_constant_p(((void *)0)) ? (
((void *)0)) : ((void *)0), .subsys_fmt_prefix = ((void *)0),
}; static const struct pi_entry *_entry_ptr __attribute__((__used__
)) __attribute__((__section__(".printk_index"))) = &_entry
; } } while (0); _printk("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
, nla_len(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]), pn_len); })
2365 nla_len(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]), pn_len)({ do { if (__builtin_constant_p("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) && __builtin_constant_p(((void *)0))) { static const
struct pi_entry _entry __attribute__((__used__)) = { .fmt = __builtin_constant_p
("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) ? ("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
) : ((void *)0), .func = __func__, .file = "drivers/net/macsec.c"
, .line = 2365, .level = __builtin_constant_p(((void *)0)) ? (
((void *)0)) : ((void *)0), .subsys_fmt_prefix = ((void *)0),
}; static const struct pi_entry *_entry_ptr __attribute__((__used__
)) __attribute__((__section__(".printk_index"))) = &_entry
; } } while (0); _printk("\001" "5" "macsec: nl: upd_txsa: bad pn length: %d != %d\n"
, nla_len(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]), pn_len); });
2366 rtnl_unlock();
2367 return -EINVAL22;
2368 }
2369
2370 spin_lock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
2371 prev_pn = tx_sa->next_pn_halves;
2372 tx_sa->next_pn = nla_get_u64(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]);
2373 spin_unlock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
2374 }
2375
2376 was_active = tx_sa->active;
2377 if (tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE])
2378 tx_sa->active = nla_get_u8(tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_ACTIVE]);
2379
2380 was_operational = secy->operational;
2381 if (assoc_num == tx_sc->encoding_sa)
2382 secy->operational = tx_sa->active;
2383
2384 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
2385 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
2386 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2387 struct macsec_context ctx;
2388
2389 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
2390 if (!ops) {
2391 ret = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2392 goto cleanup;
2393 }
2394
2395 ctx.sa.assoc_num = assoc_num;
2396 ctx.sa.tx_sa = tx_sa;
2397 ctx.sa.update_pn = !!prev_pn.full64;
2398 ctx.secy = secy;
2399
2400 ret = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_upd_txsa, &ctx);
2401 if (ret)
2402 goto cleanup;
2403 }
2404
2405 rtnl_unlock();
2406
2407 return 0;
2408
2409cleanup:
2410 if (tb_sa[MACSEC_SA_ATTR_PN]) {
2411 spin_lock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
2412 tx_sa->next_pn_halves = prev_pn;
2413 spin_unlock_bh(&tx_sa->lock);
2414 }
2415 tx_sa->active = was_active;
2416 secy->operational = was_operational;
2417 rtnl_unlock();
2418 return ret;
2419}

2513static int macsec_upd_rxsc(struct sk_buff *skb, struct genl_info *info)
2514{
2515 struct nlattr **attrs = info->attrs;
2516 struct net_device *dev;
2517 struct macsec_secy *secy;
2518 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc;
2519 struct nlattr *tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_MAX + 1];
2520 unsigned int prev_n_rx_sc;
2521 bool was_active;
2522 int ret;
2523
2524 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX])
2525 return -EINVAL22;
2526
2527 if (parse_rxsc_config(attrs, tb_rxsc))
2528 return -EINVAL22;
2529
2530 if (!validate_add_rxsc(tb_rxsc))
2531 return -EINVAL22;
2532
2533 rtnl_lock();
2534 rx_sc = get_rxsc_from_nl(genl_info_net(info), attrs, tb_rxsc, &dev, &secy);
2535 if (IS_ERR(rx_sc)) {
2536 rtnl_unlock();
2537 return PTR_ERR(rx_sc);
2538 }
2539
2540 was_active = rx_sc->active;
2541 prev_n_rx_sc = secy->n_rx_sc;
2542 if (tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE]) {
2543 bool new = !!nla_get_u8(tb_rxsc[MACSEC_RXSC_ATTR_ACTIVE]);
2544
2545 if (rx_sc->active != new)
2546 secy->n_rx_sc += new ? 1 : -1;
2547
2548 rx_sc->active = new;
2549 }
2550
2551 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
2552 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
2553 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2554 struct macsec_context ctx;
2555
2556 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
2557 if (!ops) {
2558 ret = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2559 goto cleanup;
2560 }
2561
2562 ctx.rx_sc = rx_sc;
2563 ctx.secy = secy;
2564
2565 ret = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_upd_rxsc, &ctx);
2566 if (ret)
2567 goto cleanup;
2568 }
2569
2570 rtnl_unlock();
2571
2572 return 0;
2573
2574cleanup:
2575 secy->n_rx_sc = prev_n_rx_sc;
2576 rx_sc->active = was_active;
2577 rtnl_unlock();
2578 return ret;
2579}
2580
2581static bool macsec_is_configured(struct macsec_dev *macsec)
2582{
2583 struct macsec_secy *secy = &macsec->secy;
2584 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
2585 int i;
2586
2587 if (secy->rx_sc)
2588 return true;
2589
2590 for (i = 0; i < MACSEC_NUM_AN4; i++)
2591 if (tx_sc->sa[i])
2592 return true;
2593
2594 return false;
2595}
2596
2597static bool macsec_needs_tx_tag(struct macsec_dev *macsec,
2598 const struct macsec_ops *ops)
2599{
2600 return macsec->offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_PHY &&
2601 ops->mdo_insert_tx_tag;
2602}
2603
2604static void macsec_set_head_tail_room(struct net_device *dev)
2605{
2606 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
2607 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
2608 int needed_headroom, needed_tailroom;
2609 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2610
2611 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, NULL((void *)0));
2612 if (ops) {
2613 needed_headroom = ops->needed_headroom;
2614 needed_tailroom = ops->needed_tailroom;
2615 } else {
2616 needed_headroom = MACSEC_NEEDED_HEADROOM(macsec_extra_len(true));
2617 needed_tailroom = MACSEC_NEEDED_TAILROOM16;
2618 }
2619
2620 dev->needed_headroom = real_dev->needed_headroom + needed_headroom;
2621 dev->needed_tailroom = real_dev->needed_tailroom + needed_tailroom;
2622}
2623
2624static int macsec_update_offload(struct net_device *dev, enum macsec_offload offload)
2625{
2626 enum macsec_offload prev_offload;
2627 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2628 struct macsec_context ctx;
2629 struct macsec_dev *macsec;
2630 int ret = 0;
2631
2632 macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
2633
2634 /* Check if the offloading mode is supported by the underlying layers */
2635 if (offload != MACSEC_OFFLOAD_OFF &&
2636 !macsec_check_offload(offload, macsec))
2637 return -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2638
2639 /* Check if the net device is busy. */
2640 if (netif_running(dev))
2641 return -EBUSY16;
2642
2643 /* Check if the device already has rules configured: we do not support
2644 * rules migration.
2645 */
2646 if (macsec_is_configured(macsec))
2647 return -EBUSY16;
2648
2649 prev_offload = macsec->offload;
2650
2651 ops = __macsec_get_ops(offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_OFF ? prev_offload : offload,
2652 macsec, &ctx);
2653 if (!ops)
2654 return -EOPNOTSUPP95;
2655
2656 macsec->offload = offload;
2657
2658 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
2659 ret = offload == MACSEC_OFFLOAD_OFF ? macsec_offload(ops->mdo_del_secy, &ctx)
2660 : macsec_offload(ops->mdo_add_secy, &ctx);
2661 if (ret) {
2662 macsec->offload = prev_offload;
2663 return ret;
2664 }
2665
2666 macsec_set_head_tail_room(dev);
2667 macsec->insert_tx_tag = macsec_needs_tx_tag(macsec, ops);
2668
2669 return ret;
2670}
2671
2672static int macsec_upd_offload(struct sk_buff *skb, struct genl_info *info)
2673{
2674 struct nlattr *tb_offload[MACSEC_OFFLOAD_ATTR_MAX + 1];
2675 struct nlattr **attrs = info->attrs;
2676 enum macsec_offload offload;
2677 struct macsec_dev *macsec;
2678 struct net_device *dev;
2679 int ret = 0;
2680
2681 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_IFINDEX])
2682 return -EINVAL22;
2683
2684 if (!attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_OFFLOAD])
2685 return -EINVAL22;
2686
2687 if (nla_parse_nested_deprecated(tb_offload, MACSEC_OFFLOAD_ATTR_MAX,
2688 attrs[MACSEC_ATTR_OFFLOAD],
2689 macsec_genl_offload_policy, NULL((void *)0)))
2690 return -EINVAL22;
2691
2692 rtnl_lock();
2693
2694 dev = get_dev_from_nl(genl_info_net(info), attrs);
2695 if (IS_ERR(dev)) {
2696 ret = PTR_ERR(dev);
2697 goto out;
2698 }
2699 macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
2700
2701 if (!tb_offload[MACSEC_OFFLOAD_ATTR_TYPE]) {
2702 ret = -EINVAL22;
2703 goto out;
2704 }
2705
2706 offload = nla_get_u8(tb_offload[MACSEC_OFFLOAD_ATTR_TYPE]);
2707
2708 if (macsec->offload != offload)
2709 ret = macsec_update_offload(dev, offload);
2710out:
2711 rtnl_unlock();
2712 return ret;
2713}
2714
2715static void get_tx_sa_stats(struct net_device *dev, int an,
2716 struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa,
2717 struct macsec_tx_sa_stats *sum)
2718{
2719 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
2720 int cpu;
2721
2722 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
2723 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
2724 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2725 struct macsec_context ctx;
2726
2727 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
2728 if (ops) {
2729 ctx.sa.assoc_num = an;
2730 ctx.sa.tx_sa = tx_sa;
2731 ctx.stats.tx_sa_stats = sum;
2732 ctx.secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
2733 macsec_offload(ops->mdo_get_tx_sa_stats, &ctx);
2734 }
2735 return;
2736 }
2737
2738 for_each_possible_cpu(cpu)for (((cpu)) = 0; ((cpu)) = find_next_bit((((((const struct cpumask
*)&__cpu_possible_mask))->bits)), (nr_cpu_ids), ((cpu
))), ((cpu)) < (nr_cpu_ids); ((cpu))++) {
2739 const struct macsec_tx_sa_stats *stats =
2740 per_cpu_ptr(tx_sa->stats, cpu)({ do { const void *__vpp_verify = (typeof((tx_sa->stats) +
0))((void *)0); (void)__vpp_verify; } while (0); ({ unsigned
long __ptr; __ptr = (unsigned long) ((typeof(*((tx_sa->stats
))) *)((tx_sa->stats))); (typeof((typeof(*((tx_sa->stats
))) *)((tx_sa->stats)))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset[(cpu
)])))); }); });
2741
2742 sum->OutPktsProtected += stats->OutPktsProtected;
2743 sum->OutPktsEncrypted += stats->OutPktsEncrypted;
2744 }
2745}
2746
2747static int copy_tx_sa_stats(struct sk_buff *skb, struct macsec_tx_sa_stats *sum)
2748{
2749 if (nla_put_u32(skb, MACSEC_SA_STATS_ATTR_OUT_PKTS_PROTECTED,
2750 sum->OutPktsProtected) ||
2751 nla_put_u32(skb, MACSEC_SA_STATS_ATTR_OUT_PKTS_ENCRYPTED,
2752 sum->OutPktsEncrypted))
2753 return -EMSGSIZE90;
2754
2755 return 0;
2756}

2758static void get_rx_sa_stats(struct net_device *dev,
2759 struct macsec_rx_sc *rx_sc, int an,
2760 struct macsec_rx_sa *rx_sa,
2761 struct macsec_rx_sa_stats *sum)
2762{
2763 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
2764 int cpu;
2765

2767 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
2768 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
2769 struct macsec_context ctx;
2770
2771 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
2772 if (ops) {
2773 ctx.sa.assoc_num = an;
2774 ctx.sa.rx_sa = rx_sa;
2775 ctx.stats.rx_sa_stats = sum;
2776 ctx.secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
2777 ctx.rx_sc = rx_sc;

3023
3024static int nla_put_secy(struct macsec_secy *secy, struct sk_buff *skb)
3025{
3026 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
3027 struct nlattr *secy_nest = nla_nest_start_noflag(skb,
3028 MACSEC_ATTR_SECY);
3029 u64 csid;
3030
3031 if (!secy_nest)
3032 return 1;
3033
3034 switch (secy->key_len) {
3035 case MACSEC_GCM_AES_128_SAK_LEN16:
3036 csid = secy->xpn ? MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_1280x0080C20001000003ULL : MACSEC_DEFAULT_CIPHER_ID0x0080020001000001ULL;
3037 break;
3038 case MACSEC_GCM_AES_256_SAK_LEN32:
3039 csid = secy->xpn ? MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_2560x0080C20001000004ULL : MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_2560x0080C20001000002ULL;
3040 break;
3041 default:
3042 goto cancel;
3043 }
3044
3045 if (nla_put_sci(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_SCI, secy->sci,
3046 MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_PAD) ||
3047 nla_put_u64_64bit(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_CIPHER_SUITE,
3048 csid, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_PAD) ||
3049 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_ICV_LEN, secy->icv_len) ||
3050 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_OPER, secy->operational) ||
3051 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_PROTECT, secy->protect_frames) ||
3052 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_REPLAY, secy->replay_protect) ||
3053 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_VALIDATE, secy->validate_frames) ||
3054 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_ENCRYPT, tx_sc->encrypt) ||
3055 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_INC_SCI, tx_sc->send_sci) ||
3056 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_ES, tx_sc->end_station) ||
3057 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_SCB, tx_sc->scb) ||
3058 nla_put_u8(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_ENCODING_SA, tx_sc->encoding_sa))
3059 goto cancel;
3060
3061 if (secy->replay_protect) {
3062 if (nla_put_u32(skb, MACSEC_SECY_ATTR_WINDOW, secy->replay_window))
3063 goto cancel;
3064 }
3065
3066 nla_nest_end(skb, secy_nest);
3067 return 0;
3068
3069cancel:
3070 nla_nest_cancel(skb, secy_nest);
3071 return 1;
3072}

3309static int macsec_dump_txsc(struct sk_buff *skb, struct netlink_callback *cb)
3310{

3314
3315 dev_idx = cb->args[0];
3316
3317 d = 0;
3318 rtnl_lock();
3319
3320 cb->seq = macsec_generation;
3321
3322 for_each_netdev(net, dev)for (dev = ({ void *__mptr = (void *)((&(net)->dev_base_head
)->next); _Static_assert(__builtin_types_compatible_p(typeof

(*((&(net)->dev_base_head)->next)), typeof(void)), "pointer type mismatch in container_of()"
); ((typeof(*dev) *)(__mptr - __builtin_offsetof(typeof(*dev)
, dev_list))); }); !list_is_head(&dev->dev_list, (&
(net)->dev_base_head)); dev = ({ void *__mptr = (void *)((

dev)->dev_list.next)), typeof(void)), "pointer type mismatch in container_of()"
); ((typeof(*(dev)) *)(__mptr - __builtin_offsetof(typeof(*(dev

3323 struct macsec_secy *secy;

3425static struct sk_buff *macsec_insert_tx_tag(struct sk_buff *skb,
3426 struct net_device *dev)
3427{
3428 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
3429 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
3430 struct phy_device *phydev;
3431 struct macsec_context ctx;
3432 int skb_final_len;
3433 int err;
3434
3435 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
3436 skb_final_len = skb->len - ETH_HLEN14 + ops->needed_headroom +
3437 ops->needed_tailroom;
3438 if (unlikely(skb_final_len > macsec->real_dev->mtu)__builtin_expect(!!(skb_final_len > macsec->real_dev->
mtu), 0)) {
3439 err = -EINVAL22;
3440 goto cleanup;
3441 }
3442
3443 phydev = macsec->real_dev->phydev;
3444
3445 err = skb_ensure_writable_head_tail(skb, dev);
3446 if (unlikely(err < 0)__builtin_expect(!!(err < 0), 0))
3447 goto cleanup;
3448
3449 err = ops->mdo_insert_tx_tag(phydev, skb);
3450 if (unlikely(err)__builtin_expect(!!(err), 0))
3451 goto cleanup;
3452
3453 return skb;
3454cleanup:
3455 kfree_skb(skb);
3456 return ERR_PTR(err);
3457}

3459static netdev_tx_t macsec_start_xmit(struct sk_buff *skb,
3460 struct net_device *dev)
3461{
3462 struct macsec_dev *macsec = netdev_priv(dev);
3463 struct macsec_secy *secy = &macsec->secy;
3464 struct pcpu_secy_stats *secy_stats;
3465 int ret, len;
3466
3467 if (macsec_is_offloaded(netdev_priv(dev))) {
3468 struct metadata_dst *md_dst = secy->tx_sc.md_dst;
3469
3470 skb_dst_drop(skb);
3471 dst_hold(&md_dst->dst);
3472 skb_dst_set(skb, &md_dst->dst);
3473
3474 if (macsec->insert_tx_tag) {
3475 skb = macsec_insert_tx_tag(skb, dev);
3476 if (IS_ERR(skb)) {
3477 DEV_STATS_INC(dev, tx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(dev)->stats.__tx_dropped);
3478 return NETDEV_TX_OK;
3479 }
3480 }
3481
3482 skb->dev = macsec->real_dev;
3483 return dev_queue_xmit(skb);
3484 }
3485
3486 /* 10.5 */
3487 if (!secy->protect_frames) {
3488 secy_stats = this_cpu_ptr(macsec->stats)({ do { const void *__vpp_verify = (typeof((macsec->stats)
+ 0))((void *)0); (void)__vpp_verify; } while (0); ({ unsigned
long __ptr; __ptr = (unsigned long) ((typeof(*(macsec->stats
)) *)(macsec->stats)); (typeof((typeof(*(macsec->stats)
) *)(macsec->stats))) (__ptr + (((__per_cpu_offset[debug_smp_processor_id
()])))); }); });
3489 u64_stats_update_begin(&secy_stats->syncp);
3490 secy_stats->stats.OutPktsUntagged++;
3491 u64_stats_update_end(&secy_stats->syncp);
3492 skb->dev = macsec->real_dev;
3493 len = skb->len;
3494 ret = dev_queue_xmit(skb);
3495 count_tx(dev, ret, len);
3496 return ret;
3497 }
3498
3499 if (!secy->operational) {
3500 kfree_skb(skb);
3501 DEV_STATS_INC(dev, tx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(dev)->stats.__tx_dropped);
3502 return NETDEV_TX_OK;
3503 }
3504
3505 len = skb->len;
3506 skb = macsec_encrypt(skb, dev);
3507 if (IS_ERR(skb)) {
3508 if (PTR_ERR(skb) != -EINPROGRESS115)
3509 DEV_STATS_INC(dev, tx_dropped)atomic_long_inc(&(dev)->stats.__tx_dropped);
3510 return NETDEV_TX_OK;
3511 }
3512
3513 macsec_count_tx(skb, &macsec->secy.tx_sc, macsec_skb_cb(skb)->tx_sa);
3514
3515 macsec_encrypt_finish(skb, dev);
3516 ret = dev_queue_xmit(skb);
3517 count_tx(dev, ret, len);
3518 return ret;
3519}

3570
3571static int macsec_dev_open(struct net_device *dev)
3572{
3573 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
3574 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
3575 int err;
3576
3577 err = dev_uc_add(real_dev, dev->dev_addr);
3578 if (err < 0)
3579 return err;
3580
3581 if (dev->flags & IFF_ALLMULTIIFF_ALLMULTI) {
3582 err = dev_set_allmulti(real_dev, 1);
3583 if (err < 0)
3584 goto del_unicast;
3585 }
3586
3587 if (dev->flags & IFF_PROMISCIFF_PROMISC) {
3588 err = dev_set_promiscuity(real_dev, 1);
3589 if (err < 0)
3590 goto clear_allmulti;
3591 }
3592
3593 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
3594 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
3595 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
3596 struct macsec_context ctx;
3597
3598 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
3599 if (!ops) {
3600 err = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
3601 goto clear_allmulti;
3602 }
3603
3604 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
3605 err = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_dev_open, &ctx);
3606 if (err)
3607 goto clear_allmulti;
3608 }
3609
3610 if (netif_carrier_ok(real_dev))
3611 netif_carrier_on(dev);
3612
3613 return 0;
3614clear_allmulti:
3615 if (dev->flags & IFF_ALLMULTIIFF_ALLMULTI)
3616 dev_set_allmulti(real_dev, -1);
3617del_unicast:
3618 dev_uc_del(real_dev, dev->dev_addr);
3619 netif_carrier_off(dev);
3620 return err;
3621}
3622
3623static int macsec_dev_stop(struct net_device *dev)
3624{
3625 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
3626 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
3627
3628 netif_carrier_off(dev);
3629
3630 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
3631 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
3632 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
3633 struct macsec_context ctx;
3634
3635 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
3636 if (ops) {
3637 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
3638 macsec_offload(ops->mdo_dev_stop, &ctx);
3639 }
3640 }
3641
3642 dev_mc_unsync(real_dev, dev);
3643 dev_uc_unsync(real_dev, dev);
3644
3645 if (dev->flags & IFF_ALLMULTIIFF_ALLMULTI)
3646 dev_set_allmulti(real_dev, -1);
3647
3648 if (dev->flags & IFF_PROMISCIFF_PROMISC)
3649 dev_set_promiscuity(real_dev, -1);
3650
3651 dev_uc_del(real_dev, dev->dev_addr);
3652
3653 return 0;
3654}

3656static void macsec_dev_change_rx_flags(struct net_device *dev, int change)
3657{
3658 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec_priv(dev)->real_dev;
3659
3660 if (!(dev->flags & IFF_UPIFF_UP))
3661 return;
3662
3663 if (change & IFF_ALLMULTIIFF_ALLMULTI)
3664 dev_set_allmulti(real_dev, dev->flags & IFF_ALLMULTIIFF_ALLMULTI ? 1 : -1);
3665
3666 if (change & IFF_PROMISCIFF_PROMISC)
3667 dev_set_promiscuity(real_dev,
3668 dev->flags & IFF_PROMISCIFF_PROMISC ? 1 : -1);
3669}

3674
3675 dev_mc_sync(real_dev, dev);

3679static int macsec_set_mac_address(struct net_device *dev, void *p)
3680{
3681 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
3682 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
3683 struct sockaddr *addr = p;
3684 u8 old_addr[ETH_ALEN6];
3685 int err;
3686
3687 if (!is_valid_ether_addr(addr->sa_data))
3688 return -EADDRNOTAVAIL99;
3689
3690 if (dev->flags & IFF_UPIFF_UP) {
3691 err = dev_uc_add(real_dev, addr->sa_data);
3692 if (err < 0)
3693 return err;
3694 }
3695
3696 ether_addr_copy(old_addr, dev->dev_addr);
3697 eth_hw_addr_set(dev, addr->sa_data);
3698
3699 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
3700 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
3701 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
3702 struct macsec_context ctx;
3703
3704 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
3705 if (!ops) {
3706 err = -EOPNOTSUPP95;
3707 goto restore_old_addr;
3708 }
3709
3710 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
3711 err = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_upd_secy, &ctx);
3712 if (err)
3713 goto restore_old_addr;
3714 }
3715
3716 if (dev->flags & IFF_UPIFF_UP)
3717 dev_uc_del(real_dev, old_addr);
3718
3719 return 0;
3720
3721restore_old_addr:
3722 if (dev->flags & IFF_UPIFF_UP)
3723 dev_uc_del(real_dev, addr->sa_data);
3724
3725 eth_hw_addr_set(dev, old_addr);
3726
3727 return err;
3728}

3810static void macsec_setup(struct net_device *dev)
3811{
3812 ether_setup(dev);
3813 dev->min_mtu = 0;
3814 dev->max_mtu = ETH_MAX_MTU0xFFFFU;
3815 dev->priv_flags |= IFF_NO_QUEUE;
3816 dev->netdev_ops = &macsec_netdev_ops;
3817 dev->needs_free_netdev = true;
3818 dev->priv_destructor = macsec_free_netdev;
3819 SET_NETDEV_DEVTYPE(dev, &macsec_type)((dev)->dev.type = (&macsec_type));
3820
3821 eth_zero_addr(dev->broadcast);
3822}

3824static int macsec_changelink_common(struct net_device *dev,
3825 struct nlattr *data[])
3826{
3827 struct macsec_secy *secy;
3828 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc;
3829
3830 secy = &macsec_priv(dev)->secy;
3831 tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
3832
3833 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA]) {
3834 struct macsec_tx_sa *tx_sa;
3835
3836 tx_sc->encoding_sa = nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA]);
3837 tx_sa = rtnl_dereference(tx_sc->sa[tx_sc->encoding_sa])({ do { static bool __attribute__((__section__(".data.unlikely"
))) __warned; if (debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled() && (!((
lockdep_rtnl_is_held()))) && debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled
() && !__warned) { __warned = true; lockdep_rcu_suspicious
("drivers/net/macsec.c", 3837, "suspicious rcu_dereference_protected() usage"
); } } while (0); ; ((typeof(*(tx_sc->sa[tx_sc->encoding_sa
])) *)((tx_sc->sa[tx_sc->encoding_sa]))); });
3838
3839 secy->operational = tx_sa && tx_sa->active;
3840 }
3841
3842 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCRYPT])
3843 tx_sc->encrypt = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCRYPT]);
3844
3845 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_PROTECT])
3846 secy->protect_frames = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_PROTECT]);
3847
3848 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI])
3849 tx_sc->send_sci = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI]);
3850
3851 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_ES])
3852 tx_sc->end_station = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ES]);
3853
3854 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCB])
3855 tx_sc->scb = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCB]);
3856
3857 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT])
3858 secy->replay_protect = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT]);
3859
3860 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION])
3861 secy->validate_frames = nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION]);
3862
3863 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE]) {
3864 switch (nla_get_u64(data[IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE])) {
3865 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_1280x0080C20001000001ULL:
3866 case MACSEC_DEFAULT_CIPHER_ID0x0080020001000001ULL:
3867 secy->key_len = MACSEC_GCM_AES_128_SAK_LEN16;
3868 secy->xpn = false;
3869 break;
3870 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_2560x0080C20001000002ULL:
3871 secy->key_len = MACSEC_GCM_AES_256_SAK_LEN32;
3872 secy->xpn = false;
3873 break;
3874 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_1280x0080C20001000003ULL:
3875 secy->key_len = MACSEC_GCM_AES_128_SAK_LEN16;
3876 secy->xpn = true;
3877 break;
3878 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_2560x0080C20001000004ULL:
3879 secy->key_len = MACSEC_GCM_AES_256_SAK_LEN32;
3880 secy->xpn = true;
3881 break;
3882 default:
3883 return -EINVAL22;
3884 }
3885 }
3886
3887 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_WINDOW]) {
3888 secy->replay_window = nla_get_u32(data[IFLA_MACSEC_WINDOW]);
3889
3890 /* IEEE 802.1AEbw-2013 10.7.8 - maximum replay window
3891 * for XPN cipher suites */
3892 if (secy->xpn &&
3893 secy->replay_window > MACSEC_XPN_MAX_REPLAY_WINDOW(((1 << 30) - 1)))
3894 return -EINVAL22;
3895 }
3896
3897 return 0;
3898}

3986
3987static void macsec_common_dellink(struct net_device *dev, struct list_head *head)
3988{
3989 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
3990 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
3991
3992 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
3993 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
3994 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
3995 struct macsec_context ctx;
3996
3997 ops = macsec_get_ops(netdev_priv(dev), &ctx);
3998 if (ops) {
3999 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
4000 macsec_offload(ops->mdo_del_secy, &ctx);
4001 }
4002 }
4003
4004 unregister_netdevice_queue(dev, head);
4005 list_del_rcu(&macsec->secys);
4006 macsec_del_dev(macsec);
4007 netdev_upper_dev_unlink(real_dev, dev);
4008
4009 macsec_generation++;
4010}
4011
4012static void macsec_dellink(struct net_device *dev, struct list_head *head)
4013{
4014 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
4015 struct net_device *real_dev = macsec->real_dev;
4016 struct macsec_rxh_data *rxd = macsec_data_rtnl(real_dev);
4017
4018 macsec_common_dellink(dev, head);
4019
4020 if (list_empty(&rxd->secys)) {
4021 netdev_rx_handler_unregister(real_dev);
4022 kfree(rxd);
4023 }
4024}

4026static int register_macsec_dev(struct net_device *real_dev,
4027 struct net_device *dev)
4028{
4029 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
4030 struct macsec_rxh_data *rxd = macsec_data_rtnl(real_dev);
4031
4032 if (!rxd) {
4033 int err;
4034
4035 rxd = kmalloc(sizeof(*rxd), GFP_KERNEL)({ ; ({ struct alloc_tag * __attribute__((__unused__)) _old =
((void *)0); typeof(kmalloc_noprof(sizeof(*rxd), ((( gfp_t)(

(___GFP_IO_BIT))) | (( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_FS_BIT
)))))) _res = kmalloc_noprof(sizeof(*rxd), ((( gfp_t)(((((1UL

(___GFP_IO_BIT))) | (( gfp_t)((((1UL))) << (___GFP_FS_BIT
))))); do {} while (0); _res; }); });
4036 if (!rxd)
4037 return -ENOMEM12;
4038

4040

4042 rxd);
4043 if (err < 0) {

4066static sci_t dev_to_sci(struct net_device *dev, __be16 port)

4117static int macsec_newlink(struct net *net, struct net_device *dev,
4118 struct nlattr *tb[], struct nlattr *data[],
4119 struct netlink_ext_ack *extack)
4120{
4121 struct macsec_dev *macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
4122 rx_handler_func_t *rx_handler;
4123 u8 icv_len = MACSEC_DEFAULT_ICV_LEN16;
4124 struct net_device *real_dev;
4125 int err, mtu;
4126 sci_t sci;
4127
4128 if (!tb[IFLA_LINK])
4129 return -EINVAL22;
4130 real_dev = __dev_get_by_index(net, nla_get_u32(tb[IFLA_LINK]));
4131 if (!real_dev)
4132 return -ENODEV19;
4133 if (real_dev->type != ARPHRD_ETHER1)
4134 return -EINVAL22;
4135
4136 dev->priv_flags |= IFF_MACSEC;
4137
4138 macsec->real_dev = real_dev;
4139
4140 if (data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_OFFLOAD])
4141 macsec->offload = nla_get_offload(data[IFLA_MACSEC_OFFLOAD]);
4142 else
4143 /* MACsec offloading is off by default */
4144 macsec->offload = MACSEC_OFFLOAD_OFF;
4145
4146 /* Check if the offloading mode is supported by the underlying layers */
4147 if (macsec->offload != MACSEC_OFFLOAD_OFF &&
4148 !macsec_check_offload(macsec->offload, macsec))
4149 return -EOPNOTSUPP95;
4150
4151 /* send_sci must be set to true when transmit sci explicitly is set */
4152 if ((data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCI]) &&
4153 (data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI])) {
4154 u8 send_sci = !!nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI]);
4155
4156 if (!send_sci)
4157 return -EINVAL22;
4158 }
4159
4160 if (data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN])
4161 icv_len = nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN]);
4162 mtu = real_dev->mtu - icv_len - macsec_extra_len(true);
4163 if (mtu < 0)
4164 dev->mtu = 0;
4165 else
4166 dev->mtu = mtu;
4167
4168 rx_handler = rtnl_dereference(real_dev->rx_handler)({ do { static bool __attribute__((__section__(".data.unlikely"
))) __warned; if (debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled() && (!((
lockdep_rtnl_is_held()))) && debug_lockdep_rcu_enabled
() && !__warned) { __warned = true; lockdep_rcu_suspicious
("drivers/net/macsec.c", 4168, "suspicious rcu_dereference_protected() usage"
); } } while (0); ; ((typeof(*(real_dev->rx_handler)) *)((
real_dev->rx_handler))); });
4169 if (rx_handler && rx_handler != macsec_handle_frame)
4170 return -EBUSY16;
4171
4172 err = register_netdevice(dev);
4173 if (err < 0)
4174 return err;
4175
4176 netdev_lockdep_set_classes(dev){ static struct lock_class_key qdisc_tx_busylock_key; static struct
lock_class_key qdisc_xmit_lock_key; static struct lock_class_key
dev_addr_list_lock_key; unsigned int i; (dev)->qdisc_tx_busylock
= &qdisc_tx_busylock_key; lockdep_init_map_type(&(&
(dev)->addr_list_lock)->dep_map, "&dev_addr_list_lock_key"
, &dev_addr_list_lock_key, 0, (&(dev)->addr_list_lock
)->dep_map.wait_type_inner, (&(dev)->addr_list_lock
)->dep_map.wait_type_outer, (&(dev)->addr_list_lock
)->dep_map.lock_type); for (i = 0; i < (dev)->num_tx_queues
; i++) lockdep_init_map_type(&(&(dev)->_tx[i]._xmit_lock
)->dep_map, "&qdisc_xmit_lock_key", &qdisc_xmit_lock_key
, 0, (&(dev)->_tx[i]._xmit_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_inner
, (&(dev)->_tx[i]._xmit_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_outer
, (&(dev)->_tx[i]._xmit_lock)->dep_map.lock_type); };
4177 lockdep_set_class(&dev->addr_list_lock,lockdep_init_map_type(&(&dev->addr_list_lock)->
dep_map, "&macsec_netdev_addr_lock_key", &macsec_netdev_addr_lock_key
, 0, (&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_inner
, (&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_outer, (
&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.lock_type)
4178 &macsec_netdev_addr_lock_key)lockdep_init_map_type(&(&dev->addr_list_lock)->
dep_map, "&macsec_netdev_addr_lock_key", &macsec_netdev_addr_lock_key
, 0, (&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_inner
, (&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.wait_type_outer, (
&dev->addr_list_lock)->dep_map.lock_type);
4179
4180 err = netdev_upper_dev_link(real_dev, dev, extack);
4181 if (err < 0)
4182 goto unregister;
4183
4184 /* need to be already registered so that ->init has run and
4185 * the MAC addr is set
4186 */
4187 if (data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCI])
4188 sci = nla_get_sci(data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCI]);
4189 else if (data && data[IFLA_MACSEC_PORT])
4190 sci = dev_to_sci(dev, nla_get_be16(data[IFLA_MACSEC_PORT]));
4191 else
4192 sci = dev_to_sci(dev, MACSEC_PORT_ES((( __be16)(__u16)__builtin_bswap16((__u16)((0x0001))))));
4193
4194 if (rx_handler && sci_exists(real_dev, sci)) {
4195 err = -EBUSY16;
4196 goto unlink;
4197 }
4198
4199 err = macsec_add_dev(dev, sci, icv_len);
4200 if (err)
4201 goto unlink;
4202
4203 if (data) {
4204 err = macsec_changelink_common(dev, data);
4205 if (err)
4206 goto del_dev;
4207 }
4208
4209 /* If h/w offloading is available, propagate to the device */
4210 if (macsec_is_offloaded(macsec)) {
4211 const struct macsec_ops *ops;
4212 struct macsec_context ctx;
4213
4214 ops = macsec_get_ops(macsec, &ctx);
4215 if (ops) {
4216 ctx.secy = &macsec->secy;
4217 err = macsec_offload(ops->mdo_add_secy, &ctx);
4218 if (err)
4219 goto del_dev;
4220
4221 macsec->insert_tx_tag =
4222 macsec_needs_tx_tag(macsec, ops);
4223 }
4224 }
4225
4226 err = register_macsec_dev(real_dev, dev);
4227 if (err < 0)
4228 goto del_dev;
4229
4230 netif_stacked_transfer_operstate(real_dev, dev);
4231 linkwatch_fire_event(dev);
4232
4233 macsec_generation++;
4234
4235 return 0;
4236
4237del_dev:
4238 macsec_del_dev(macsec);
4239unlink:
4240 netdev_upper_dev_unlink(real_dev, dev);
4241unregister:
4242 unregister_netdevice(dev);
4243 return err;
4244}
4245
4246static int macsec_validate_attr(struct nlattr *tb[], struct nlattr *data[],
4247 struct netlink_ext_ack *extack)
4248{
4249 u64 csid = MACSEC_DEFAULT_CIPHER_ID0x0080020001000001ULL;
4250 u8 icv_len = MACSEC_DEFAULT_ICV_LEN16;
4251 int flag;
4252 bool es, scb, sci;
4253
4254 if (!data)
4255 return 0;
4256
4257 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE])
4258 csid = nla_get_u64(data[IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE]);
4259
4260 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN]) {
4261 icv_len = nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN]);
4262 if (icv_len != MACSEC_DEFAULT_ICV_LEN16) {
4263 char dummy_key[DEFAULT_SAK_LEN16] = { 0 };
4264 struct crypto_aead *dummy_tfm;
4265
4266 dummy_tfm = macsec_alloc_tfm(dummy_key,
4267 DEFAULT_SAK_LEN16,
4268 icv_len);
4269 if (IS_ERR(dummy_tfm))
4270 return PTR_ERR(dummy_tfm);
4271 crypto_free_aead(dummy_tfm);
4272 }
4273 }
4274
4275 switch (csid) {
4276 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_1280x0080C20001000001ULL:
4277 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_2560x0080C20001000002ULL:
4278 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_1280x0080C20001000003ULL:
4279 case MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_2560x0080C20001000004ULL:
4280 case MACSEC_DEFAULT_CIPHER_ID0x0080020001000001ULL:
4281 if (icv_len < MACSEC_MIN_ICV_LEN8 ||
4282 icv_len > MACSEC_STD_ICV_LEN16)
4283 return -EINVAL22;
4284 break;
4285 default:
4286 return -EINVAL22;
4287 }
4288
4289 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA]) {
4290 if (nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA]) >= MACSEC_NUM_AN4)
4291 return -EINVAL22;
4292 }
4293
4294 for (flag = IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA + 1;
4295 flag < IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION;
4296 flag++) {
4297 if (data[flag]) {
4298 if (nla_get_u8(data[flag]) > 1)
4299 return -EINVAL22;
4300 }
4301 }
4302
4303 es = data[IFLA_MACSEC_ES] ? nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_ES]) : false;
4304 sci = data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI] ? nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI]) : false;
4305 scb = data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCB] ? nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_SCB]) : false;
4306
4307 if ((sci && (scb || es)) || (scb && es))
4308 return -EINVAL22;
4309
4310 if (data[IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION] &&
4311 nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION]) > MACSEC_VALIDATE_MAX)
4312 return -EINVAL22;
4313
4314 if ((data[IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT] &&
4315 nla_get_u8(data[IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT])) &&
4316 !data[IFLA_MACSEC_WINDOW])
4317 return -EINVAL22;
4318
4319 return 0;
4320}

4339static size_t macsec_get_size(const struct net_device *dev)
4340{
4341 return nla_total_size_64bit(8) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_SCI */
4342 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN */
4343 nla_total_size_64bit(8) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE */
4344 nla_total_size(4) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_WINDOW */
4345 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA */
4346 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_ENCRYPT */
4347 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_PROTECT */
4348 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI */
4349 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_ES */
4350 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_SCB */
4351 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT */
4352 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION */
4353 nla_total_size(1) + /* IFLA_MACSEC_OFFLOAD */
4354 0;
4355}
4356
4357static int macsec_fill_info(struct sk_buff *skb,
4358 const struct net_device *dev)
4359{
4360 struct macsec_tx_sc *tx_sc;
4361 struct macsec_dev *macsec;
4362 struct macsec_secy *secy;
4363 u64 csid;
4364
4365 macsec = macsec_priv(dev);
4366 secy = &macsec->secy;
4367 tx_sc = &secy->tx_sc;
4368
4369 switch (secy->key_len) {
4370 case MACSEC_GCM_AES_128_SAK_LEN16:
4371 csid = secy->xpn ? MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_1280x0080C20001000003ULL : MACSEC_DEFAULT_CIPHER_ID0x0080020001000001ULL;
4372 break;
4373 case MACSEC_GCM_AES_256_SAK_LEN32:
4374 csid = secy->xpn ? MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_XPN_2560x0080C20001000004ULL : MACSEC_CIPHER_ID_GCM_AES_2560x0080C20001000002ULL;
4375 break;
4376 default:
4377 goto nla_put_failure;
4378 }
4379
4380 if (nla_put_sci(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_SCI, secy->sci,
4381 IFLA_MACSEC_PAD) ||
4382 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_ICV_LEN, secy->icv_len) ||
4383 nla_put_u64_64bit(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_CIPHER_SUITE,
4384 csid, IFLA_MACSEC_PAD) ||
4385 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_ENCODING_SA, tx_sc->encoding_sa) ||
4386 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_ENCRYPT, tx_sc->encrypt) ||
4387 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_PROTECT, secy->protect_frames) ||
4388 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_INC_SCI, tx_sc->send_sci) ||
4389 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_ES, tx_sc->end_station) ||
4390 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_SCB, tx_sc->scb) ||
4391 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_REPLAY_PROTECT, secy->replay_protect) ||
4392 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_VALIDATION, secy->validate_frames) ||
4393 nla_put_u8(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_OFFLOAD, macsec->offload) ||
4394 0)
4395 goto nla_put_failure;
4396
4397 if (secy->replay_protect) {
4398 if (nla_put_u32(skb, IFLA_MACSEC_WINDOW, secy->replay_window))
4399 goto nla_put_failure;
4400 }
4401
4402 return 0;
4403
4404nla_put_failure:
4405 return -EMSGSIZE90;
4406}

4418 .get_size = macsec_get_size,

4420 .get_link_net = macsec_get_link_net,
4421};

4427
4428static int macsec_notify(struct notifier_block *this, unsigned long event,
4429 void *ptr)
4430{
4431 struct net_device *real_dev = netdev_notifier_info_to_dev(ptr);
4432 LIST_HEAD(head)struct list_head head = { &(head), &(head) };
4433
4434 if (!is_macsec_master(real_dev))
